CNN-based detectors extract local features efficiently but cannot suppress noise-induced false alarms without global acoustic context. Transformer-based methods capture long-range dependencies at quadratic computational cost. Existing Mamba-based vision models offer efficient linear-cost scanning but lack multi-scale semantic alignment across pyramid levels, multi-receptive-field fusion, and small-target-aware training supervision needed for reliable sonar detection.
  This letter proposes Mamba Dilated-Scale Fusion (MambaDSF), a hybrid framework addressing these limitations through three contributions: a Mamba Enhanced Feature Pyramid (MambaEFP) backbone that jointly captures local echo cues and global acoustic context at linear complexity; a Dilate Fusion Mamba (DFMamba) encoder that enforces multi-scale feature alignment across pyramid levels; and Scale-Adaptive Weighted IoU (SA-WIoU) and Cross-Scale Coherence (CSC) losses that stabilize small-target training. MambaDSF achieves 91.5% mAP50 on the UATD forward-looking sonar benchmark with 28.7 million parameters, surpassing all compared detectors. On a small-target subset the gain reached +2.2 percentage points, and cross-domain evaluation on FLS and MD-FLS confirms the generalization of the proposed architecture. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/IDontKnowAAA/MambaDSF.

</details>


### [39] [Towards Large Model Feature Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24025)
*Youwei Pang,Changsheng Gao,Dong Liu,Huchuan Lu,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对大模型特征编码（LaMoFC）的基准评测框架LaMoFCBench，涵盖4大类16个场景，揭示了现有编码范式与大模型异质特征之间的严重不匹配，呼吁建立新的编码范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在实际部署中面临计算/内存预算和隐私约束，分割执行虽能缓解这些约束，但引入了中间特征的大量传输与存储问题。现有特征编码方法主要针对CNN的同质空间激活图，而大模型生成的是具有不同统计分布和压缩容忍度的异质特征（如多级/多模态表示、自回归上下文缓存），亟需一个系统的评估框架。

Method: 首先构建了LaMoFCBench特征数据集，覆盖4大类16个场景，整合了广泛采用的架构和多种分割计算设置；然后根据实际应用场景指定代表性分割点以提取中间特征，建立统一的评测管线；最后对主流通用特征编解码器进行基准测试。

Result: 现有编码范式与大模型异质特征之间存在严重不匹配，证明了LaMoFC需要从根本上脱离现有范式。

Conclusion: LaMoFCBench为驱动这一范式转变提供了共享的实证基础，数据与代码将开源。

Abstract: Large models have delivered remarkable performance across a wide range of perception and generation tasks, yet practical deployment is increasingly constrained by computational and memory budgets, as well as privacy requirements. Split execution alleviates these constraints by partitioning computation across devices, but it inevitably introduces intensive transmission and storage of intermediate features. Unlike conventional feature coding for CNNs that typically targets homogeneous spatial activation maps, modern large models generate heterogeneous features with varying statistical distributions and compression tolerances, e.g., multi-level/multi-modal representations and autoregressive context caches. These characteristics necessitate treating large model feature coding (LaMoFC) as a fundamental system component and call for a systematic evaluation framework. In this paper, we present a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation framework for LaMoFC. We first build the feature dataset LaMoFCBench, covering diverse task requirements across 4 categories and 16 scenarios while integrating widelyadopted architectures and various split-computing settings. We then specify representative split points according to practical application scenarios to extract intermediate features, establishing a unified pipeline for fair and reproducible comparisons. Finally, we benchmark mainstream universal feature codecs, exposing the profound misalignment between existing coding paradigms and the heterogeneous nature of large model features. These findings reveal that LaMoFC demands a fundamental departure from existing paradigms, and LaMoFCBench provides the shared empirical foundation to drive this transition. The data and code will be available at https://github.com/lartpang/LaMoFCBench.

</details>


### [40] [Towards Open-World Referring Expression Comprehension: A Benchmark with Training-free Multi-task Consistency Checker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25706)
*Zongjian Wu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenRef是一个面向开放世界复杂场景的指代表达理解（REC）新基准，涵盖多样视觉场景（地面/无人机/夜间/恶劣天气）、多目标及无目标样本、丰富词汇类型（专有名词/多义词/序数词），并提出N3R指标评估负样本拒绝可靠性以及MCC策略来增强模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REC基准仅包含简单场景且假设每个表达唯一对应一个目标，限制了模型在开放世界环境中的部署。OpenRef旨在填补这一空白，提供更贴近真实世界的复杂场景评估。

Method: 1) 构建包含多种视觉域、可变目标数量、丰富词汇类型的基准；2) 提出N3R指标评估负样本拒绝可靠性；3) 提出MCC（多任务一致性检查器），一种即插即用、无需训练的自我验证策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，OpenRef显著提升了现有REC模型在复杂场景下的性能，为开放世界REC铺平了道路。

Conclusion: OpenRef通过更全面的基准设计和评估指标，以及MCC策略，有效推动了REC模型向开放世界场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Referring expression comprehension (REC) aims to localize a target object within an image based on a given expression. Although recent advances in vision-language models have led to substantial improvements in REC tasks, current REC benchmarks often hold simple scenarios and the assumption that each expression maps to a unique object. These limitations hinder the deployment of REC models in open-world environments. To fill this gap, we introduce OpenRef, a new benchmark for REC in complex visual and linguistic scenarios. OpenRef features three key advancements: 1) Diverse visual scenarios: spanning diverse visual domains, including ground views, drone views, dark scenes and adverse weather conditions; 2) Variable target counts: breaking the single-target limitation with multi-target and none-target samples; 3) Rich vocabulary types: incorporating proper nouns, polysemous words and ordinal terms to fit a wider range of expression needs. Furthermore, as traditional metrics are insufficient for open-world setting, we leverage F1 to measure grounding accuracy and propose N3R (Negative Relative Rejection Reliability) to assess relative rejection reliability against negative expressions. Finally, we introduce Multi-task Consistency Checker (MCC), a training-free but plug-and-play strategy that enhances model performance with one click by enforcing consistency self-verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this work significantly advances the performance of existing REC models in complex scenarios, paving the way for open-world REC. Project page: https://zongjianwu.github.io/openref

</details>


### [41] [Tetris: Tile-level Sampling for Efficient and High-Fidelity Video Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25538)
*Chanwut Kittivorawong,Alena Chao,Charlie Si,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tetris是一个面向静态视频的轨道提取系统，通过基于多联骨牌的瓦片数据模型实现细粒度时空剪枝，在保持5%精度损失的前提下，相比先前系统实现最高17.4倍吞吐量提升，相比全帧参考管线实现最高68.8倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道物化系统通过时间帧采样降低成本，但会擦除细粒度跟踪所需的帧间运动信息；而静态视频中大部分区域不包含兴趣目标，且不同区域可容忍不同的采样率，现有方法未能有效利用这一特性进行剪枝。

Method: 将视频分解为基于瓦片的多联骨牌数据模型，并运行三个上游算子：分类器识别相关瓦片并聚合成多联骨牌；整数线性规划(ILP)在用户指定精度约束下剪枝冗余多联骨牌；打包器将幸存多联骨牌组装成画布以最小化检测器调用次数。

Result: 在7个静态视频数据集上，Tetris在5%跟踪精度损失范围内保持性能，而先前系统在3/7数据集上超过该界限；在此界限下，Tetris吞吐量比先前系统高17.4倍，比参考管线高68.8倍。

Conclusion: Tetris通过细粒度时空剪枝策略有效降低了静态视频中轨道提取的计算成本，同时保持了高保真度，在精度和效率之间取得了优于现有方法的平衡。

Abstract: Track materialization converts raw video into reusable object tracks that downstream queries can run against without rerunning tracking, but extracting those tracks efficiently and with high fidelity remains expensive. Prior systems reduce cost through temporal frame sampling, erasing the inter-frame motion that fine-grained tracking requires. In stationary video, however, large portions of each frame contain no objects of interest, and the remaining regions tolerate different sampling rates. We present Tetris, a track-extraction system that decomposes videos into a tile-based polyomino data model, enabling fine-grained spatiotemporal pruning that reduces detector calls with minimal fidelity loss. Tetris runs three operators upstream of the user-provided detector: a classifier identifies relevant tiles and groups them into polyominoes, an integer linear program (ILP) prunes redundant polyominoes under a user-specified accuracy constraint, and a packer assembles the survivors into canvases that minimize detector calls. Across 7 stationary-video datasets, Tetris stays within a 5% tracking accuracy loss of a full-frame, every-frame reference pipeline, whereas prior systems exceed this bound on 3 of the 7 datasets. At this 5% bound, Tetris achieves up to 17.4x higher throughput than prior systems and up to 68.8x higher than the reference pipeline. The project page is at https://tetris-db.github.io .

</details>


### [42] [Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting for Adversarial Robustness of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25922)
*Xiao Liu,Jiaxiang Liu,Boci Peng,Boren Hu,Yusong Wang,Xiwen Chen,Prayag Tiwari,Liming Zhang,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出闭环双向提示（Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting）方法，通过动态反馈循环恢复跨模态语义对齐，引入语义锚点作为稳定先验约束循环更新，在11个数据集上实现了SOTA鲁棒性和基到新类泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗防御方法多为单向或静态的，未能利用双向跨模态互补性和实例自适应的保护机制，导致视觉语言模型在面对对抗扰动时跨模态语义对齐被破坏且无法有效恢复。

Method: 在冻结编码器上构建闭环双向提示框架：1）引入语义锚点作为稳定先验约束循环更新，缓解扰动导致的特征退化；2）通过锚点引导的引导学习，文本语义去噪视觉表示；3）精炼后的视觉表示实现实例自适应的提示更新，形成矫正后的鲁棒共识。

Result: 在11个数据集上的广泛评估验证了该方法达到了SOTA鲁棒性，具备强大的基到新类泛化能力，并在计算成本与准确率之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 闭环双向提示通过动态反馈机制和语义锚点约束，有效克服了单向和静态防御的局限，实现了跨模态语义对齐的鲁棒恢复。

Abstract: Vision Language Models adapt well to downstream tasks but are highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations that disrupt cross-modal semantic alignment. Existing defenses are largely unidirectional or structural, failing to exploit bidirectional cross-modal complementarity and instance-wise adaptive protection. To overcome the limitations of unidirectional and static defenses in adversarial settings, we propose Closed-Loop Bidirectional Prompting, casting robust adaptation as cross-modal agreement recovery via a dynamic feedback loop on frozen encoders. A Semantic Anchor is introduced as a stable prior to constrain cyclic updates and mitigate perturbation-induced feature corruption. Through anchor-based bootstrapping, textual semantics denoise visual representations, while the refined visuals enable instance-adaptive prompt updating, yielding a rectified and robust consensus. Extensive evaluations across 11 datasets validate state-of-the-art robustness and strong base-to-new generalization, while maintaining a favorable trade-off between computational cost and accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [CoDA: Color Distribution Probing for Efficient and Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24306)
*Zexi Jia,Zhiqiang Yuan,Xiaoyue Duan,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou,Anil K. Jain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeForm基准（37万图像、62领域）和基于颜色分布探测的轻量级检测器CoDA（1.48M参数），通过噪声量化探针捕捉生成图像的颜色非均匀性伪影，在跨领域评估中达到SOTA，兼顾泛化性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测面临泛化性（轻量方法跨生成器/领域退化）与效率（大模型计算昂贵）的权衡，且缺乏跨领域鲁棒性评估基准。

Method: 提出FakeForm大规模基准（62领域、37万图像）覆盖跨模型与跨领域评估；设计CoDA检测器，基于噪声量化探针（Noise-Quantization Probe）提取颜色分布特征，利用真实照片颜色模式更平滑稳定、合成图像存在颜色失衡的观察。

Result: CoDA（仅1.48M参数）在标准基准上达到SOTA，在FakeForm跨领域评估中取得最佳结果，在跨模型真实感场景中保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: 生成图像中持续存在的颜色伪影可作为高效鲁棒检测的实用基础，轻量级颜色分析策略在跨领域泛化中优于现有方法。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection faces a persistent trade-off between generalization and efficiency: lightweight artifact-based methods often degrade on unseen generators or domains, whereas more robust large-scale models are computationally expensive. Meanwhile, existing benchmarks mainly focus on cross-model evaluation in photorealistic settings, leaving cross-domain robustness underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce FakeForm, a large-scale benchmark with approximately 370,000 images across 62 diverse domains for both cross-model and cross-domain evaluation. Motivated by this broader setting, we revisit color-distribution probing as an efficient complementary cue for AI-generated image detection. We observe that, especially for photographic content, real photographs tend to exhibit smoother and more stable color patterns, whereas synthetic images often show characteristic color imbalances introduced by neural generation. Based on this observation, we propose CoDA, a compact 1.48M-parameter detector built on a Noise-Quantization Probe, together with a theoretical analysis linking probe responses to color non-uniformity. Experiments show that CoDA achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks and the best results on the challenging cross-domain evaluation of FakeForm, while remaining highly competitive in cross-model photorealistic settings. These results suggest that persistent generative artifacts can provide a practical foundation for efficient and robust AI-generated image detection. The models and FakeForm benchmark will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [44] [ControlLight: Towards Controllable, Consistent, and Generalizable Low-Light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25569)
*Yufeng Yang,Jianzhuang Liu,Jisheng Chu,Yuqi Peng,Xianfang Zeng,Jiancheng Huang,Shifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ControlLight框架，通过构建大规模连续光照强度监督数据集和错位感知加权流匹配损失，实现了低光图像增强的可控性、一致性和泛化性，在多项指标上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的低光增强方法受限于小规模数据集和单一增强目标，导致在实际场景中泛化能力差且缺乏可控性。

Method: 1) 构建了带有连续光照强度监督的大规模真实世界退化图像数据集；2) 提出错位感知加权流匹配损失，在不同控制强度下保持图像结构一致性；3) 用户可灵活控制增强强度，实现满意增强效果。

Result: 在多项实验上达到SOTA性能，展现出强大的连续可控性以及对真实世界场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ControlLight在低光图像增强的可控性、一致性和泛化性方面优于现有方法，为实际应用提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing deep learning-based low-light enhancement methods are typically trained on limited datasets with single enhancement targets, which restricts their generalization ability and controllability in real-world applications. To overcome these limitations, we propose ControlLight, a controllable, consistent, and generalizable framework for low-light enhancement. We first construct a large-scale dataset of real-world degraded images with continuous illumination-strength supervision. To further ensure consistent outputs under different control strengths, we introduce a misalignment-aware weighted flow matching loss that preserves image structure across continuous enhancement strengths. ControlLight allows users to edit real-world degraded low-light images toward satisfactory enhancement results by flexibly controlling the strength while preserving visual consistency and realism. Extensive experiments show that ControlLight achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing low-light enhancement approaches while demonstrating strong continuous controllability and generalization to real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [45] [Event-to-Video Reconstruction using Spatio-Temporal and Frequency-Enhanced Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25804)
*Ramna Maqsood,Paulo Nunes,Luís Ducla Soares,Caroline Conti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多尺度频率增强Transformer的MSFET-E2V模型，通过跨域注意力模块融合时空特征与小波变换频率特征，并设计轻量级小波增强跳跃块，在多个真实事件数据集上实现了优于现有方法的事件到视频重建性能，同时大幅降低了参数量、GPU内存消耗和推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CNN和Transformer的E2V重建方法主要在空间域操作，难以恢复精细结构细节并抑制严重重建伪影，需要一种能同时捕捉局部与全局结构、增强细节保留和鲁棒性的新方法。

Method: 提出了MSFET-E2V多尺度频率增强Transformer模型，核心是跨域注意力模块，融合时空特征与离散小波变换导出的频率感知表示；同时设计了轻量级小波增强跳跃块作为跳跃连接，通过联合空间-频率域处理促进伪影抑制和结构细节优化。

Result: 在多个真实世界事件数据集上，MSFET-E2V在重建质量上优于现有最先进方法，并且相比现有基于Transformer的方法，显著减少了参数量、GPU内存使用和推理时间。

Conclusion: 引入频率域信息（离散小波变换）能有效增强事件到视频重建的质量，且多尺度频率增强设计在降低计算开销的同时提升了细节保留和伪影抑制能力。

Abstract: Event cameras offer significant advantages over conventional frame-based counterparts, including high temporal resolution, low latency, and energy efficiency. These characteristics make them suitable for high-speed and high-dynamic range scene acquisition scenarios; however, the lack of dense intensity frames limits the direct applicability of conventional computer vision methods for scene understanding. Event-to-video (E2V) reconstruction seeks to bridge this gap by converting asynchronous event streams into a sequence of synchronous video frames. Existing E2V reconstruction methods based on convolutional neural networks and transformers operate primarily in the spatial domain and often struggle to recover fine structural details while suppressing severe reconstruction artifacts. To address these issues, we propose MSFET-E2V, a novel multiscale frequency-enhanced transformer model. At its core lies a cross-domain attention module, which fuses spatio-temporal features with frequency-aware representations derived from the discrete wavelet transform. Unlike prior methods relying solely on spatial attention, our approach effectively captures both local and global structures by taking into account low- and high-frequency components, enhancing detail preservation and robustness across various motion scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a lightweight wavelet-enhanced skip block that serves as a skip connection, facilitating artifact suppression and structural detail refinement through joint spatial-frequency domain processing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSFET-E2V achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world event datasets, offering significant gains in reconstruction quality. Moreover, compared to the existing transformer-based method, our proposed model significantly reduces the number of parameters, the GPU memory usage, and inference time.

</details>


### [46] [Full-4D: Generating Full-Scope 4D Scenes from a Single-View Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25500)
*Tingxi Chen,Ke Hao,Yabo Chen,Zhengxue Cheng,Rong Xie,Li Song,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种从单视角视频生成完整4D场景的框架，通过多视角视频合成+优化4D重建实现，贡献包括：大规模多视角视频数据集Real-MV-4D、融合时间-视图注意力的扩散模型、以及流匹配蒸馏损失用于4D表示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角视频生成4D场景是高度不适定问题，现有方法受限于单目视频、简单3D效果或微小视角扰动，无法实现真正的4D生成；同时缺乏大规模全范围4D场景数据集。

Method: 1) 构建Real-MV-4D多视角视频数据集；2) 训练基于融合时间-视图(T×V)注意力机制的多视角视频扩散模型，直接嵌入几何重投影先验和显式相机条件；3) 将合成多视角视频提升为显式4D表示(4DGS)，并用流匹配蒸馏损失进行正则化。

Result: 在视觉保真度和几何一致性上优于现有方法，实现了从单视角视频生成全范围4D场景。

Conclusion: 通过多视角视频合成+4D重建的两阶段范式，结合大规模数据集和创新的注意力机制，有效解决了单视角视频到4D场景的生成问题。

Abstract: Generating 4D scenes from a single-view video is inherently ill-posed: a single viewpoint lacks the information needed to recover a complete, dynamic scene with full coverage. Existing methods are typically limited to monocular videos, simple 3D effects, or only small viewpoint perturbations around the original viewpoint, falling short of true 4D generation. Meanwhile, the lack of large-scale datasets capturing full-scope 4D scenes with synchronized multi-view videos further hinders progress in this direction. We propose a novel single-view video-to-4D framework that casts full-scope 4D generation as a multi-view video synthesis followed by optimization-based 4D reconstruction from the generated views. To instantiate this formulation end-to-end, we make three key contributions. First, we introduce Real-MV-4D, a large-scale dataset of synchronized multi-view videos captured in diverse real-world environments to provide the 4D supervision. Second, we train a multi-view video diffusion model driven by a novel fused time(T)-view(V) attention mechanism that directly embeds geometric reprojection priors and explicit camera conditioning into its view-time interactions. Unlike basic feature fusion, this direct binding strictly aligns the generation process with physical 3D priors to produce a dense, synchronized T$\times $V video grid. Third, rather than relying on non-interactive and inconsistent 2D video interpolations, we lift the synthesized multi-view videos into an explicit 4D representation (i.e. 4DGS), regularized by a Flow Matching Distillation loss that exploits the multi-view prior to improve novel-view rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both visual fidelity and geometric consistency, enabling full-scope 4D scene generation from single-view videos.

</details>


### [47] [Rethinking Scribble-Guided Image Editing: Generalization, Instruction Adherence, and Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25568)
*Mingyi Xu,Jinpeng Lin,Min Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Ming Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现涂鸦引导图像编辑中指令级泛化比图像域泛化更具挑战性，并提出覆盖-真实感课程学习、多任务拼接和编辑聚焦损失三种策略，在VIBE基准上实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涂鸦引导编辑模型在多任务场景下性能不稳定，本文通过实证研究揭示性能瓶颈主要在于模型对多样化编辑指令的学习不足，而非图像域间隙，从而针对性提出改进策略。

Method: (a) 覆盖-真实感课程：先构建大规模合成指令丰富数据实现广泛任务覆盖，再用少量真实数据精调生成真实感；(b) 多任务拼接：零成本拼接单任务样本构造多任务训练数据，且学到的能力可泛化到非拼接图像；(c) 编辑聚焦损失：利用合成数据中输入输出图像的差异区域，聚焦训练编辑区域，提升学习效率和编辑精度。

Result: 在VIBE基准上，单任务和多任务涂鸦引导编辑均取得显著提升，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 指令级泛化是当前涂鸦引导编辑的主要瓶颈，所提三种策略有效缓解了该问题，将开源数据集和模型以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Scribble-guided image editing allows users to combine simple scribble annotations with text prompts to specify both where and how an image should be edited, enabling flexible interaction with precise spatial control. However, existing models still exhibit unstable performance under this paradigm, especially in multi-task scenarios. To improve performance, we conduct empirical studies using an open-source editing model and reveal an asymmetry in generalization: instruction-level generalization, including across editing tasks and from single-task to multi-task settings, is more challenging than image-domain generalization, such as from synthetic to real-world images or from mosaicked to regular images. This suggests that the primary bottleneck lies in insufficient learning for diverse editing instructions rather than in the image domain gap. Motivated by this insight, we propose three strategies: (a) a Coverage-then-Realism Curriculum, a two-stage pipeline that first builds large-scale synthetic, instruction-rich data for broad task supervision, then curates a small set of real-world data to refine generation realism; (b) Multi-Task Mosaicking, which constructs multi-task training samples by concatenating single-task examples at nearly zero cost while enabling the learned capability to generalize to non-mosaicked images; and (c) an Edit-Focused Loss, which leverages the changed regions between input and output images in synthetic data to focus training on edited regions, improving both learning efficiency and editing accuracy. With these strategies, we substantially improve both single-task and multi-task scribble-guided editing on the VIBE benchmark, achieving state-of-the-art results. We will publicly release our dataset and model.

</details>


### [48] [StreamOV: Streaming Omni-Video Understanding via Evidence-Guided Memory and Response Triggering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25621)
*Ming Xie,Zizheng Huang,Xudong Tan,Chao Wang,Xiangyu Zeng,Wenxiao Wu,Tao Chen,Limin Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StreamOV框架，通过多模态证据引导的长短期记忆和隐状态驱动触发机制，实现流式全模态视频理解，并构建首个在线多轮全模态评测基准SOVBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态方法仅适用于离线场景，无法处理持续增长的音视频上下文，且缺乏自主响应触发能力；同时缺乏面向连续多轮流式交互的评测基准。

Method: 1) 多模态证据引导的长短期记忆（证据压缩+固定预算管理）；2) 隐状态驱动的响应触发机制（无需显式静默标记或外部路由器）。

Result: 在多个流式及全模态视频基准上达到最先进性能，验证了在线和离线视频理解的有效性。

Conclusion: StreamOV有效弥合了全模态方法在流式场景中的缺口，为在线音视频推理提供了高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: While streaming omni-video understanding demands continuous perception and proactive, real-time interaction, this crucial area remains largely under-explored. Current omni-modal methods are inherently designed for offline settings, limiting their applicability in streaming scenarios due to two fundamental flaws. First, they lack robust mechanisms to manage continuously growing audio-visual context over long horizons and cannot autonomously initiate responses at opportune moments. Second, existing benchmarks are predominantly confined to offline, single-turn question answering, failing to capture continuous, multi-turn streaming interactions. To bridge these gaps, we propose StreamOV, a novel Streaming Omni-Video understanding framework for efficient online audio-visual reasoning with bounded memory and proactive response triggering. Specifically, StreamOV introduces a multimodal evidence-guided long-short term memory that condenses historical audio-visual context into compact informative evidence under a fixed budget. It further employs a hidden-state-driven trigger to decide when to respond, avoiding explicit silence-token generation and external routers. We also curate SOVBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for online, multi-turn omni-modal evaluation. Extensive experiments show that StreamOV achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse streaming and omni-video benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness for both online and offline video understanding.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Active Real-to-Twin Inspection: A New Paradigm for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25407)
*Jiaxuan Liu,Yunkang Cao,Yufeng Chen,Chunyang Li,Yuhuan Du,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本异常检测新任务Real-to-Twin AD，通过CAD数字孪生与真实观测的几何对齐进行异常检测；并设计AVATAR框架，利用无缺陷配对样本弥合Sim2Real域差异，实现零样本异常定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测依赖被动固定视角的2D图像，无法适应实际工业检测中主动、动态的观测需求，严重制约其在具身工业检测场景中的应用。

Method: 提出AVATAR框架：1) 基于无缺陷的Real与CAD Twin配对样本，学习两者间的鲁棒语义对齐；2) 利用CAD先验生成动态无缺陷参考；3) 将异常检测转化为检测不可对齐的偏差，实现零样本异常定位。

Result: 在大量实验中，AVATAR显著优于适配后的最先进基线方法，对剧烈的视角变化表现出极强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过Real-to-Twin AD任务和AVATAR框架，突破了传统2D图像被动视角的局限，为具身工业检测中的零样本异常检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The deployment of zero-shot anomaly detection (AD) in embodied industrial inspection is severely bottlenecked by its reliance on passive, fixed-viewpoint 2D imagery. Such formulations inherently fail to accommodate the active, dynamic observations required in real-world environments. To break this limitation, we introduce Real-to-Twin Anomaly Detection, a novel task that evaluates physical observations directly against geometrically matched CAD Digital Twins. To tackle this new task, we propose AVATAR, a framework designed to learn robust semantic alignment between Real and Digital Twins. By bridging benign Sim2Real domain gaps using only defect-free pairs, AVATAR effectively transforms CAD priors into dynamic, anomaly-free references. This elegant formulation enables the model to localize diverse anomalies in a zero-shot manner as unalignable deviations, eliminating the need for defect annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AVATAR substantially outperforms adapted state-of-the-art baselines, exhibiting exceptional robustness to severe viewpoint variations. The code and dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [50] [World Models as Group Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24578)
*Zijie Wang,Wei Zhang,Weiming Zhang,Fanqi Zhang,Xiao Tan,Yipeng Qin,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于群论的动作忠实度评估框架，通过潜在空间正则化方法强化视频世界模型中的动作-状态对应关系，并引入GAC和GAR两个新指标来评估动作一致性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽视觉逼真度高，但其动态演化未必真正受动作控制，缺乏对动作忠实度的系统性评估标准。

Method: 将动作条件世界模型形式化为状态空间上的群作用，通过潜在空间正则化强制执行恒等、逆和组合一致性约束，并引入GAC和GAR两个评估指标。

Result: 所提方法在多个SOTA视频世界模型上一致提升了GAC和GAR指标，且未降低感知质量。

Conclusion: 基于群论的结构化正则化是提升世界模型动作忠实度的有效途径，为超越视觉质量的动态评估提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Video world models have achieved strong visual realism, but this does not ensure that their dynamics are truly governed by actions. In this work, we argue that action faithfulness should be understood through the compositional structure of actions, which in many embodied settings follows a group structure (e.g., SE(2) for navigation). Based on this insight, we formalize action-conditioned world modeling as realizing a group action on the state space, providing a principled criterion for evaluating dynamics beyond visual quality. To operationalize this framework, we propose a unified approach that enforces identity, inverse, and composition consistency via latent-space regularization with synthesized supervision, avoiding additional data collection. We further introduce two metrics: Group-Action Consistency (GAC) and Group-Action Robustness (GAR), to evaluate structural correctness and rollout stability. Extensive experimental results show that our method consistently improves both GAC and GAR in state-of-the-art video world models without degrading perceptual quality.

</details>


### [51] [Hierarchical Consistency Learning for Test-time Adaptation in Camouflage Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25651)
*Mingfeng Zha,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Yunqiang Pei,Chaofan Qiao,Jiening Zhang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次一致性学习(HCL)框架，通过测试时自适应和层次表示重建、任务亲和引导、原型一致性校准等机制，解决伪装目标检测中的域刚性和标注依赖问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法受限于静态的"训练-冻结"范式，存在域刚性和标注依赖性，难以适应场景变化和未见过的伪装模式。

Method: 1) 层次表示重建(HRR)：结合空间重建与双流频域分解缓解特征纠缠；2) 任务亲和引导(TAG)：通过通道级亲和力跨分支传播知识，对齐局部判别性线索；3) 原型一致性校准(PCC)：聚合区域特征为紧凑原型，建立原型-特征相似性，施加隐式层次约束。

Result: 在四个伪装目标和四个水下目标数据集上，三种退化设置下，均一致优于现有最先进方法，展现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HCL框架通过测试时自适应和层次一致性学习有效解决了域刚性和标注依赖问题，为COD提供了更鲁棒和泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to localize targets that exhibit minimal perceptual differences from backgrounds through physical attributes. Existing methods, constrained by the static train-then-freeze paradigm, suffer from domain rigidity and annotation dependency, limiting their adaptability to scene variations and unseen camouflage patterns. To overcome these, we propose the hierarchical consistency learning (HCL) framework, which integrates test-time adaptation for dynamic representation recalibration. Specifically, we design the hierarchical representation reconstruction (HRR) to alleviate feature entanglement by synergizing spatial reconstruction with dual-stream frequency-domain decomposition, enhancing robustness against appearance homogenization. The pixel and spectrum inference provide structural and contextual priors. We further introduce task affinity guidance (TAG) to propagate knowledge across branches via channel-wise affinity, aligning local discriminative cues and mitigating semantic drift. To ensure semantic invariance, we formulate the prototype consistency calibration (PCC), which aggregates region features into compact prototypes and establishes prototype-feature similarity. This imposes implicit and hierarchical constraints that bridge task and representation gaps. Extensive experiments across four camouflaged and four underwater object benchmarks, under three degradation settings, demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, highlighting its robustness and generalization under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [52] [Physen-Noise2Noise: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Defocus Deblurring with Bias Correction under Low-Light Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24590)
*Ziyan Huang,Lang Wu,Hongji Wang,Yifei Liu,Dongliang Tang,Hongqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Physen-Noise2Noise，一种基于物理模型的去散焦模糊自监督框架，通过频域约束和可学习噪声偏置参数处理复杂有偏噪声，无需干净参考图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光长曝光散焦去模糊中同时存在严重模糊和复杂有偏噪声，现有方法依赖简化的噪声假设，在真实成像条件下效果有限。

Method: 提出物理模型引导的自监督去模糊框架Physen-Noise2Noise，包含：1) 推导散焦成像过程固有的频域约束；2) 引入可学习噪声偏置参数显式建模有偏噪声；3) 多帧噪声初始化策略抑制复杂有偏噪声；4) 预训练-微调变体增强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，该方法在存在复杂有偏噪声的散焦去模糊任务中持续优于现有最先进的自监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模有偏噪声并将其融入自监督学习框架，Physen-Noise2Noise有效解决了传统Noise2Noise方法在零均值噪声假设下的局限，提升了复杂噪声条件下的去模糊性能。

Abstract: Low-light, long-exposure defocus deblurring remains a challenging problem due to the simultaneous presence of severe blur and complex biased noise. Existing methods typically rely on simplified noise assumptions, which limits their effectiveness under realistic imaging conditions. In this work, we propose Physen-Noise2Noise, a self-supervised deblurring framework guided by the physical model of defocus imaging, which leverages noisy multi-frame observations without requiring clean reference images. Unlike conventional Noise2Noise-based approaches that assume zero-mean noise, we derive a frequency-domain constraint inherent to the defocus imaging process and incorporate it into the learning framework via a learnable noise bias parameter. In addition, a multi-frame noisy initialization strategy is introduced to suppress complex biased noise prior to deblurring, providing a more stable starting point for reconstruction. This formulation explicitly models biased noise and enables joint bias correction and high-frequency detail recovery during training. Furthermore, we develop a pretrain-finetune variant to enhance robustness and generalization under challenging noise conditions. Extensive experiments on both simulation and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art self-supervised approaches for defocus deblurring in the presence of complex biased noise.

</details>


### [53] [Channel-wise Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26089)
*Wei Song,Tianhang Wang,Yitong Chen,Tong Zhang,Zuxuan Wu,Ming Li,Jiaqi Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通道级向量量化（CVQ）取代传统补丁级向量量化，将图像表示为离散的视觉细节层级，并基于此提出通道级自回归（CAR）模型，通过"下一通道预测"机制按通道顺序生成图像，先勾勒全局结构再细化细节，在图像重建和文本到图像生成任务上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统向量量化（VQ）将每个补丁特征向量分配为离散令牌，将图像视为空间补丁网格，这种方式限制了模型对全局结构和细节层级的建模能力。本文旨在设计一种新的图像分词范式，能够更好地捕捉图像的视觉细节层级结构，并实现更高效的码本利用。

Method: (1) 提出通道级向量量化（CVQ）：对特征图的每个通道进行量化，将图像表示为离散的视觉细节层级，而非空间补丁网格。(2) 提出通道级自回归（CAR）模型：采用"下一通道预测"机制，按通道顺序依次预测图像通道，先预测全局结构通道，再预测细粒度属性通道，模拟人类艺术家的创作流程。

Result: (1) CVQ在16K+码本大小下实现100%码本利用率，且无需额外技巧，重建质量显著优于传统VQ；(2) CAR在文本到图像生成任务上取得DPG得分86.7和GenEval得分0.79，展示了强大的有效性。

Conclusion: 通道级分词（CVQ）和通道级自回归（CAR）框架是一种有效的图像生成新范式，通过按视觉细节层级建模和生成图像，优于传统的空间补丁级方法，为视觉自回归模型提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We present Channel-wise Vector Quantization (CVQ), a novel image tokenization paradigm that replaces patch-wise tokens with channel-wise tokens. Unlike conventional vector quantization, which assigns a discrete token to each patch feature vector, CVQ quantizes each channel of the feature map. This formulation represents an image as discrete levels of visual details, rather than as a grid of spatial patches. Based on CVQ, we introduce a new visual autoregressive framework with "next-channel prediction". Instead of rendering images patch by patch in raster order, our Channel-wise Autoregressive (CAR) model predicts image channels sequentially, producing progressively enriched visual details. Specifically, it first sketches global structure and then refines fine-grained attributes, akin to a human artist's workflow. Empirically, we show that: (1) CVQ achieves 100% codebook utilization with a 16K+ codebook size without any bells and whistles, and substantially improves reconstruction quality over conventional VQ; and (2) CAR attains a DPG score of 86.7 and a GenEval score of 0.79, demonstrating strong effectiveness for text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [54] [LLaVA-OneVision-2: Towards Next-Generation Perceptual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25979)
*Xiang An,Yin Xie,Feilong Tang,Yunyao Yan,Huajie Tan,Didi Zhu,Changrui Chen,Xiuwei Zhao,Bin Qin,Kaicheng Yang,Yifei Shen,Yuanhan Zhang,Kaichen Zhang,Wenkang Zhang,Zheng Cheng,Nansen Zhang,Chunsheng Wu,Chunjiang Ge,Zimin Ran,Dehua Song,Chunyuan Li,Shikun Feng,Ming Hu,Zhangquan Chen,Junbo Niu,Bo Li,Ziyong Feng,Ziwei Liu,Zongyuan Ge,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LLaVA-OV-2 基于原生 OneVision 编码器，提出码流令牌化（codec-stream tokenization）方法，将压缩视频视为连续比特流，通过比特成本动态划分自适应时间组、运动残差线索选择显著空间证据，并用共享 3D RoPE 建立统一时空坐标，在有限令牌预算下实现高效长视频压缩。配合约1200万样本的大规模开放监督数据训练，在视频理解、时空定位、操纵追踪等任务上全面超越 Qwen3-VL-8B，JumpScore 上 mAP 达74.9，领先44.8点，视频任务平均+4.3点，空间任务+5.3点，追踪任务J&F+15.6点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理长视频时存在令牌效率低下、时间建模不充分的问题，固定图像组压缩方式难以适应视频内容动态变化；同时缺乏针对高频密集重复运动场景的精细时间定位评估基准。

Method: 码流令牌化：将压缩视频视为连续比特成本流，根据比特成本动态划分自适应时间组；利用运动残差线索选择显著空间证据，构建紧凑视觉画布；通过共享 3D RoPE 将码流画布、采样帧和图像统一到同一时空坐标系中；采用窗口注意力（Windowed Attention）实现高效局部计算并保持原生分辨率。

Result: JumpScore 基准上 8B 模型 mAP 达 74.9，超 Qwen3-VL-8B 的 30.1 达 44.8 点；相同令牌预算下码流输入比帧采样时间定位提升 9.7 点；标准基准上视频任务平均超 4.3 点，空间任务超 5.3 点，追踪任务 J&F 超 15.6 点。

Conclusion: 码流令牌化方法通过感知编码比特成本动态和运动残差，实现了比固定图像组更稳定的长视频令牌压缩；大规模开放监督数据训练进一步提升了模型的多模态基准表现；JumpScore 基准有效补充了现有视频评估在高频密集重复运动场景下的空白。

Abstract: We introduce LLaVA-OneVision-2 (LLaVA-OV-2), the most capable vision-language model in the LLaVA-OneVision series to date, achieving superior performance across a broad range of multimodal benchmarks. The model builds on a native OneVision-Encoder and incorporates Windowed Attention for efficient local computation while maintaining native resolution. Its key advance is codec-stream tokenization: it treats compressed video as a continuous bit-cost stream, where bit-cost dynamics determine adaptive temporal groups, and motion-residual cues select salient spatial evidence into compact visual canvases. This allocation concentrates a limited token budget on event-bearing content, enabling more stable long-video token compression than fixed groups of pictures. A shared 3D RoPE further places codec canvases, sampled frames, and images in a unified spatiotemporal coordinate system. Furthermore, we build the LLaVA-OV-2 data and training stack around large-scale open supervision: approximately 8M re-captioned video samples for pretraining, a 4M-sample spatial corpus for fine-tuning. We also introduce JumpScore, a temporal-localization benchmark targeting fine-grained grounding in high-frequency, densely repeated motion, a regime underrepresented by existing video evaluations. A standout capability of LLaVA-OV-2 is its unified perception across video understanding, temporal grounding, spatial grounding, and manipulation-trace reasoning. On JumpScore, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B reaches 74.9 JumpScore mAP, surpassing Qwen3-VL-8B (30.1) by +44.8 points; under matched visual-token budgets on the same benchmark, codec-stream inputs improve temporal grounding over frame sampling by +9.7 points. Across standard benchmarks, LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B further outperforms Qwen3-VL-8B by +4.3 average points on video tasks, +5.3 on spatial tasks, and +15.6 average J&F on tracking tasks.

</details>


### [55] [Understanding the Impact of Geometric Foundation Models on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24642)
*Yurou Yang,Muyuan Lin,Roberto Martin-Martin,Martin Labrie,Shreekant Gayaka,Cheng-Hao Kuo,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了将几何基础模型（GFM）注入视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的最佳策略，通过线性探针量化了VLA与GFM之间的"几何鸿沟"，比较了三种注入架构，并分析了非架构因素（训练数据、相机数量、重建质量）对几何VLA性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将几何基础模型与VLA结合的工作虽然性能有所提升，但仍不清楚三个关键问题：(i)现代VLA本身是否已具备足够的几何理解能力；(ii)将几何理解注入VLA的最佳架构是什么；(iii)其他设计选择对几何VLA有何影响。本文旨在通过严格的实验分析回答这些问题。

Method: 以GR00T-N1.5（VLA）和VGGT（GFM）为具体对象，首先通过线性探针（linear probing）量化VLA与GFM之间的"几何鸿沟"；然后实现三种不同的架构策略将GFM与VLA融合，在保持底层实现细节尽可能一致的前提下进行公平比较；最后分析非架构因素（训练数据、相机数量、重建质量）的影响。

Result: 首次量化了VLA与GFM之间的几何鸿沟；识别并比较了三种不同的几何注入架构；分析了训练数据、相机数量、重建质量等非架构因素对几何VLA性能的影响。

Conclusion: 通过系统实验分析，厘清了VLA的几何理解现状、最佳几何注入架构以及非架构设计因素对几何VLA性能的影响，为该领域提供了重要的实验依据和设计指导。

Abstract: Recent work explores new opportunities at the intersection of vision-language-action models (VLAs) and geometric foundation models (GFMs) for 3D reconstruction, such as VGGT. While the resulting geometric VLAs often show improved performance, it remains unclear (i) if modern VLAs already have sufficient geometric understanding to start with, (ii) what is the best architecture to inject geometric understanding into a VLA, and (iii) what is the effect of other design choices that affect geometric VLAs. In this paper we provide a rigorous experimental analysis to shed light on these questions, for a specific choice of VLA (GR00T-N1.5) and GFM (VGGT). Our first contribution is to formalize prior work's intuition that current VLAs lack geometric understanding, by providing a rigorous analysis based on linear probing. The analysis quantifies, for the first time, the "geometric gap" between VLAs and GFMs. Our second contribution is to identify and compare different strategies to bridge GFMs with VLAs. We implement three different architectures, which differ in the way they inject geometry in the VLA, while keeping low-level implementation details as similar as possible, to ensure a fair comparison. Finally, we analyze the impact of non-architectural choices (e.g., training data, number of cameras, reconstruction quality) on the performance of the geometric VLAs.

</details>


### [56] [CMAP: Cross-Modal Adaptive Prompting for Multi-Domain Task-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25708)
*Sriram Mandalika*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模态文本空间增强的视觉-语言模型持续学习方法，通过文本空间任务路由、多原型视觉-文本置信度评估和对称跨模态门控三个创新，在无需任务身份和少量参数（2.5M）下，在11数据集1201类的MTIL基准上达到74.2%转移、80.5%平均、88.7%最后一任务的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于冻结视觉-语言模型的参数高效持续学习方法仅依赖视觉特征进行任务路由、置信度估计和编码器适配，完全未利用CLIP的跨模态文本嵌入空间，限制了模型在多领域任务增量学习中的性能。

Method: 1) 文本空间任务路由：用冻结CLIP文本原型的余弦相似度替代视觉高斯匹配，实现零参数、顺序无关的任务路由；2) 多原型视觉-文本置信度：用K均值视觉原型和跨模态对齐分数替代单高斯类建模，结合任务校准阈值；3) 对称跨模态门控：将逐层Gumbel门扩展到文本编码器，以批量图像特征为条件，保持OOD输入上的跨模态对齐。

Result: 在MTIL基准（11数据集、1201类）Order-I设置下，Transfer达74.2%、Average达80.5%、Last达88.7%，分别超越SOTA 5.0、3.7、3.0个百分点，仅2.5M可训练参数且无需外部数据。

Conclusion: 利用CLIP的跨模态文本嵌入空间能够有效提升多领域任务增量学习的性能，证明了视觉-语言模型中未被充分利用的文本模态信息在持续学习场景中的关键价值。

Abstract: Multi-domain task-incremental learning requires a model to sequentially acquire knowledge across visually diverse domains without forgetting prior tasks, and without access to task identity at inference. Parameter-efficient methods built on frozen vision-language models have made strong progress, yet all existing approaches rely exclusively on visual features for task routing, confidence estimation, and encoder adaptation, leaving CLIP's cross-modal text embedding space entirely unexploited. We address this gap through three contributions. Text-space task routing replaces visual Gaussian matching with cosine similarity to frozen CLIP text prototypes, giving order-independent routing robust to data scarcity at zero parameter cost. Multi-prototype visual-textual confidence replaces single-Gaussian class modeling with K-means visual prototypes and cross-modal alignment scores under task-calibrated thresholds. Symmetric cross-modal gating extends per-layer Gumbel gates to the text encoder conditioned on batch image features, preserving cross-modal alignment on out-of-distribution inputs. On the MTIL benchmark spanning 11 datasets and 1201 classes, our method achieves 74.2% Transfer, 80.5% Average, and 88.7% Last under Order-I, surpassing the prior state of the art by 5.0, 3.7, and 3.0 percentage points with only 2.5M trainable parameters and no external data.

</details>


### [57] [AnE: Pushing the Reasoning Frontier of Multimodal LLMs via Anchor Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25571)
*Zehao Wang,Yihan Zeng,Zidong Gong,Yuanfan Guo,Feng Zhu,Hongzhi Zhang,Wei Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anchor Evolution (AnE)范式，通过真值锚点数据筛选与Scaffold-Stripping机制，解决多模态大模型后训练中的认知漂移和幻觉推理路径问题，在8个多模态基准上平均提升10.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT和RL后训练范式受限于静态数据的性能瓶颈，自反思/自进化方法因低质量合成数据导致认知漂移和幻觉推理路径。

Method: 1) Truth Anchor Expansion: 通过轨迹展开定位模型失败前沿，利用真实数据库检索高保真锚点进行数据筛选；2) Scaffold-Stripping Mechanism: 先用支架增强监督锚定推理路径以缓解学习复杂度和分布漂移，再通过RL剥离支架模板将推理路径内化为模型能力。

Result: 在8个多模态推理基准上平均提升10.3%，达到当前最优结果。

Conclusion: AnE范式通过真值锚定数据筛选和渐进式支架剥离，实现了多模态推理能力忠实且稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Post-training via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) is crucial for enhancing reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), yet existing paradigms often reach a performance bottleneck due to the limitations of static data. While current methods leverage self-reflection or self-evolution to push these boundaries, they still suffer from cognitive drift and hallucinated reasoning paths caused by low-quality synthetic data. To address these challenges, we propose Anchor Evolution (AnE), a new paradigm that integrates truth-anchored data curation and model evolution, achieving faithful and steady performance gains at the reasoning frontier. Specifically, we propose Truth Anchor Expansion, which pinpoints the model failing frontier via trajectory rollouts and leverages ground-truth databases to retrieve high-fidelity anchors for faithful data curation. Subsequently, we introduce the Scaffold-Stripping Mechanism to internalize reasoning capabilities. This mechanism first anchors reasoning paths via scaffold-augmented supervision to mitigate the learning complexity and distribution drift of direct SFT on raw data, then leverages RL to strip the scaffold template, thereby effectively transitioning the reasoning paths into intrinsic model capabilities. Experimental results on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that our method substantially advances the model performance frontier, improving the base model by 10.3\% across eight multimodal benchmarks and achieving state-of-the-art results. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [58] [Single View Seafloor Recovery from Imaging Sonar via Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24195)
*Sevan Brodjian,Michael Hobley,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的、基于可微分渲染的单视角声纳图像水深恢复方法，能在30秒内从单张前视成像声纳图像中恢复海底地形，在分布偏移下优于监督CNN，且无需训练数据即可适应不同传感器配置和环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水下地形测绘方法通常需要多视角、昂贵多传感器设置或大量训练数据，限制了其适用性和对新环境的适应能力。作者希望提出一种无需训练、高效的单视角方法来解决这一难题。

Method: 通过可微分声纳光线追踪，在已知海底倾斜条件下，优化显式高度场以再现目标声纳图像，实现从单张前视成像声纳图像中恢复水深。

Result: 在合成数据集上，该方法在分布偏移下优于监督CNN，在粗糙地形上与CNN性能接近；CNN在分布内数据上表现更优。该方法能适应不同传感器配置和环境。

Conclusion: 这是首个面向声纳的单视角高度恢复可微分渲染方法，通过物理驱动的先验建模实现了无需训练数据的泛化能力，为水下地形测绘提供了高效且适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Sonar is often the only modality suitable for high-resolution imaging underwater due to light attenuation and turbidity. Forward-looking imaging sonar provides measurements over range and horizontal angle but collapses vertical structure into a flat image, creating ambiguities that make 3D recovery challenging. A common use case for imaging sonar is underwater terrain mapping (bathymetry), yet current methods require many views, expensive multi-sensor setups, or significant training data, which limits use and adaptability to new environments.
  We present a training-free method that recovers bathymetry from a single sonar image in under 30 seconds via differentiable rendering, conditioned on a known seafloor tilt. To our knowledge, this is the first differentiable rendering approach for single-view height recovery in sonar. Our method implements differentiable sonar ray tracing and optimizes an explicit height field to reproduce the target image. On synthetic datasets, our approach outperforms a supervised CNN under distribution shift and remains close on rough terrain, while the CNN wins in-distribution. By modeling physically grounded priors of the sonar process, our method adapts across sensor configurations and environments without training data.

</details>


### [59] [Where Detectors Fail: Probing Generative Space for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24906)
*Zijie Cao,Weijie Tu,Yao Xiao,Weijian Deng,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE框架，通过将检测器作为评论家引导生成器进行流形级修改，主动探索生成过程中的困难区域，生成难以分类的样本用于改进检测器，从而提升AI生成图像检测的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在应对未见过的生成器时泛化能力不足，根本原因在于训练数据对生成变化的覆盖有限，单纯扩大数据规模无法解决这一问题。

Method: 提出PROBE框架，利用检测器作为评论家来引导生成器进行流形级修改，主动探索生成过程中具有挑战性的区域，生成标准采样策略下难以遇到的困难样本，并用这些样本优化检测器。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，PROBE能够提升检测器对未见生成器的泛化能力，获得更可泛化的AIGI检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过主动探索生成过程中的边界区域并生成困难样本，可以有效提升AIGI检测器的泛化性能，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated images (AIGI) remains challenging because detectors often fail to generalize to unseen generators. Although existing methods are trained on large datasets, their performance still degrades when generation settings change, indicating that data scale alone is insufficient and that limited coverage of generative variations during training is a key factor. Studies on generative model editing show that small changes in internal representations can produce diverse and meaningful image variations, many of which are not explored under standard sampling. Leveraging this insight, we propose PROBE (Probing Robustness via Boundary Exploration), a framework that improves detector generalization by actively exploring challenging regions of the generative process. Instead of treating the generator as a fixed data source, PROBE uses the detector as a critic to steer the generator through manifold-level modifications, producing realistic samples that are difficult to classify. These samples expose failure cases that are uncommon under standard data sampling strategies and are used to refine the detector. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks indicate that PROBE enhances generalization to unseen generators, resulting in more generalizable AIGI detection performance. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Amamiya-C/PROBE-AIGI-Detection

</details>


### [60] [CLIP-Guided SAM: Parameter-Efficient Semantic Conditioning for Promptable Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24807)
*Shayan Jalilian,Abdul Bais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIP-Guided SAM，通过轻量级多模态语义适配器将CLIP的文本、视觉和相似度特征直接注入SAM的图像编码器，实现内部语义条件化，在低标注数据场景下支持手动和半自动两种模式，性能优于或媲美现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM虽然能生成高质量掩码，但语义盲视，依赖外部提示指定类别；现有VLM+SAM方法通过外部提示耦合（两阶段）解决该问题，但效率不高。本文旨在通过内部语义条件化实现参数高效的语义引导分割。

Method: 将CLIP提取的文本、视觉和相似度特征通过轻量级多模态语义适配器注入SAM图像编码器，在内部层面对特征表示进行语义条件化，同时保留SAM原有的可提示接口。支持手动模式（文本+空间提示）和半自动文本模式（仅文本输入）。

Result: 在通用基准和下游任务上，CLIP-Guided SAM在低标注数据设置下，相比SAM+PEFT基线、VLM+SAM流水线、SAM 3以及依赖大量无标签数据的半监督分割方法，取得了优于或具有竞争力的性能，同时保持参数高效。

Conclusion: 内部语义条件化比外部提示耦合更有效；训练与推理时的提示一致性是重要的设计原则；该方法在参数高效的同时适用于标注数据稀缺的场景。

Abstract: Promptable foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) produce high-quality masks but remain semantically blind, relying on external prompts to specify categories. Existing vision-language approaches address this limitation by using external prompt coupling, where a vision-language model generates spatial prompts for SAM as a separate stage.
  We propose CLIP-Guided SAM, a parameter-efficient segmentation framework built on internal semantic conditioning. Instead of using semantic signals only to generate prompts, we inject CLIP-derived text, vision, and similarity features directly into SAM's image encoder through lightweight multi-modal semantic adapters. These adapters condition SAM's internal feature representations, allowing semantic information to influence mask prediction while preserving SAM's original promptable interface.
  Our framework is designed for low labeled-data settings and applies to both general-domain benchmarks and specialized downstream tasks. It supports two operating modes: Manual mode, for interactive segmentation with both text and spatial prompts, and Semi-Automatic text-only mode, for applications that require concept-specific segmentation using only textual input. We show that robustness depends on aligning training with the type of prompts used at inference, making train-test prompt consistency an important design principle.
  Through extensive experiments and ablations, we evaluate our method against SAM+PEFT baselines without semantic conditioning, vision-language + SAM pipelines, SAM 3, and strong semi-supervised segmentation methods that rely on large amounts of unlabeled data. Across these settings, CLIP-Guided SAM consistently achieves superior or competitive performance while remaining parameter-efficient in both training and deployment.

</details>


### [61] [Divide-and-Conquer Inference for Large-Scale Visual Recognition with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24799)
*Zhipeng Ye,Jiaqi Huang,Feng Jiang,Qiufeng Wang,Yikang Duan,Dawei Wang,Xihang Zhou,Qian Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在大规模图像分类中因标签空间扩大而出现的性能崩溃现象，并从信息论角度归因于信息熵与注意力稀释/衰减之间的冲突。为此提出分治推理（DCI）策略，通过递归分解分类任务和动态剪枝机制，在无需额外训练的情况下显著提升长序列推理精度与速度，使轻量级开源模型性能媲美甚至超越前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在大规模图像分类中性能随标签空间扩大而显著下降（即长序列识别中的性能崩溃现象），现有方法缺乏对此问题的系统分析及有效解决方案。

Method: 提出Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI)方法，通过递归地将全局分类任务分解为多个更简单的局部子问题，并采用动态剪枝机制压缩搜索空间，从而改善长序列推理中的信噪比，同时降低自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-21K等基准测试上，DCI一致提升了分类精度，使得轻量级开源模型无需训练或微调即可媲美甚至超越前沿闭源模型，并实现了更优的计算缩放行为和更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: DCI作为一种模型无关、即插即用的测试时缩放策略，为大规模场景下MLLMs推理精度的扩展提供了一种高效范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across a wide range of vision language tasks. However, when applied to large scale image classification, their performance degrades significantly as the label space expands a phenomenon we define as Performance Collapse in Long Sequence Recognition. Through an information theoretic analysis, we reveal that this collapse stems from a fundamental conflict between the escalating information entropy and the prominent attention dilution and decay within attention mechanisms, which impairs the model's ability to maintain a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio when processing extremely long prompts. To mitigate this, we propose Divide-and-Conquer Inference (DCI), a novel test-time scaling strategy for visual recognition with MLLMs. DCI recursively decomposes complex global classification tasks into multiple simpler, localized subproblems and employs a dynamic pruning mechanism to compress the search space. This method effectively improves the local signal to noise ratio and model accuracy by mitigating the inherent weight dilution issues in long-sequence inference. Moreover, while traditional self-attention incurs a prohibitive quadratic computational complexity, DCI achieves more favorable scaling behavior and substantially accelerates inference in large scale classification scenarios. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-21K demonstrate that DCI consistently improves classification accuracy. This enables lightweight open-source models to rival or even surpass frontier closed-source giants without any additional training or fine-tuning. As a model-agnostic, plug-and-play paradigm, DCI offers an efficient approach for scaling the inferential precision of MLLMs in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [62] [Enhancing Single-Image Facial Demorphing using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25442)
*Nitish Shukla,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）引导的耦合扩散重建框架，用于无参考面部去变形，通过利用MLLM中间层的语义嵌入作为条件信号，在RGB域中联合合成两个原始人脸图像，解决了现有方法无法恢复原始身份信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部变形攻击检测方法只能识别变形图像，无法恢复原始图像或身份信息，限制了取证用途。

Method: 利用MLLM中间层提取语义嵌入作为条件信号，引导耦合扩散模型在RGB域中联合重建两个原始人脸，避免有损的文本生成-重新编码循环。

Result: RGB域去变形在严格操作点下比潜空间方法提升30-40%；中间MLLM层编码更具身份判别性的表征；完整MLLM嵌入优于原始ViT特征。

Conclusion: 所提无参考去变形框架能够有效恢复变形图像的原始身份信息，为面部取证提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Face recognition systems are increasingly vulnerable to morphing attacks, where a composite image is crafted to match multiple identities, enabling unauthorized access and identity fraud. Existing detection methods identify morphed images but cannot recover constituent images or identities, limiting their forensic utility. This paper presents a novel reference-free facial demorphing framework that leverages Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to guide a coupled diffusion-based reconstruction process. Our key innovation lies in extracting semantic embeddings from intermediate MLLM layers to condition the demorphing, providing high-level reasoning about facial attributes and identity cues that complement low-level pixel information. We formulate demorphing as a coupled conditional generation problem, where both constituent faces are synthesized jointly through a denoising diffusion model operating directly in the RGB domain, ensuring inter-identity consistency while preserving fine-grained perceptual details. Unlike prior approaches that rely on compressed latent representations or assume identity overlap between training and testing sets, our method bypasses lossy text generation-reencoding cycles by directly utilizing MLLM hidden states as conditioning signals, enabling the denoising network to attend to subtle visual cues such as hair, background, and facial textures. Ablation studies further reveal that middle MLLM layers encode more identity-discriminative representations, RGB-domain demorphing outperforms latent-space approaches by 30--40\% at strict operating points, and full MLLM embeddings provide substantial advantages over raw ViT features through enhanced semantic structuring from multimodal pretraining.

</details>


### [63] [Metric--Phase Fields: Decoupling Distance and Sign for Thin-Structure Reconstruction from Unoriented Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25503)
*Jiayi Kong,Xuhui Chen,Chen Zong,Fei Hou,Junhui Hou,Wenping Wang,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Metric-Phase Fields（MPFs），一种解耦隐式表示方法，通过分离度量距离（metric proximity）与拓扑相位（topological phase）来同时处理薄结构、开放边界和水密流形。MPFs学习无符号度量场r和光滑相位场θ，并通过门控度量公式与残差相位注入得到稳定的有符号隐式函数。实验表明，MPFs在薄壳和薄板形状上优于现有SDF和UDF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号距离函数（SDFs）在重建水密流形上表现出色，但由于严格的内部-外部约束，无法处理薄结构和开放边界；而无符号距离场（UDFs）虽能处理一般几何，但在零水平集处存在梯度奇异性，导致优化和提取困难。现有方法无法同时兼顾两类几何的表示能力与训练稳定性。

Method: 提出Metric-Phase Fields (MPFs)，包含：(i) 无符号度量场 r 和 (ii) 光滑相位场 θ。推导有界相位指示器 P=tanh(βθ) 提供软内部-外部线索。通过门控度量公式和残差相位注入将两个场耦合为有符号隐式函数。相位系数 β 可学习，自适应控制相位过渡的锐度和软符号指示器的饱和程度。

Result: 在合成和扫描的薄壳、薄板形状上的实验表明，MPFs在保持薄结构和分层结构上比现有基于SDF的方法更忠实，同时比基于UDF的方法训练更鲁棒、表面提取更可靠。

Conclusion: MPFs通过解耦度量与相位，有效弥合了SDFs与UDFs之间的差距，实现了对复杂几何（包括薄结构和开放边界）的鲁棒重建，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Neural Signed Distance Functions (SDFs) excel at reconstructing watertight manifolds but fail on thin structures and open boundaries due to strict inside--outside constraints. Conversely, Unsigned Distance Fields (UDFs) accommodate general geometries but suffer from gradient singularities at the zero-level set, hindering optimization and extraction. We introduce Metric--Phase Fields (MPFs), a decoupled implicit representation that separates metric proximity from topological phase. Given an unoriented point cloud, MPFs learn (i) an unsigned metric field $r$ and (ii) a smooth phase field $θ$, for which we derive a bounded phase indicator $P=\tanh(βθ)$ that provides soft inside--outside cues where they are meaningful. We couple the two fields via a gated-metric formulation with a residual phase injection to obtain a signed implicit function with stable near-surface gradients. The phase coefficient $β$ is learnable, allowing MPFs to adaptively control the sharpness of the phase transition and the degree of saturation of the soft sign indicator. Experiments on both synthetic and scanned thin-shell and thin-plate shapes demonstrate that MPFs preserve thin and layered structures more faithfully than recent SDF-based methods, while also enabling more robust training and more reliable surface extraction than UDF-based approaches. Check out \href{https://github.com/JIAYI-Scarlett/ICML2026-MPF}{MPFs-GitHub} for source code and test models.

</details>


### [64] [MIND: Multi-Scale Intent Diffusion for Text-Driven Physics-Based Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26006)
*Bin Li,Ruichi Zhang,Han Liang,Jingyan Zhang,Juze Zhang,Xin Chen,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND框架，一种基于行为意图作为语义桥梁的端到端扩散模型，用于文本驱动的物理仿真人形机器人控制，通过多尺度意图扩散机制实现从高级文本命令到低级动作的语义对齐和物理合理的行为生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两类问题：(1) 两阶段范式（运动生成+物理跟踪）存在领域偏移；(2) 端到端模仿学习范式面临文本命令与低级动作之间的巨大模态鸿沟，难以实现有效的语义对齐。人形机器人状态编码了丰富的运动动力学信息，比低级动作更具语义对齐性，可作为行为意图的自然基础。

Method: 提出MIND框架：(1) 以行为意图作为文本命令与低级动作之间的语义桥梁；(2) 多尺度意图扩散机制：整体意图预测器捕获全局行为动力学指导整体行为合成，即时意图预测器提供逐步骤细粒度信号进行局部行为细化；(3) 将人形机器人状态编码到潜在空间以实现更有效的语义意图建模。

Result: 大量实验表明MIND优于现有方法，能够从文本命令合成连贯、物理合理且语义对齐的人形机器人行为。

Conclusion: MIND通过层次化意图公式化，为人形机器人控制引入了结构化归纳偏置，提升了语义对齐和行为自然度，为文本驱动的物理仿真人形机器人控制提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Enabling physics-based humanoids to execute diverse behaviors from high-level textual commands remains a significant challenge. Existing methods typically follow either a two-stage paradigm that combines kinematic motion generation with physics-based tracking, or an end-to-end imitation-learning paradigm that directly generates actions from text. However, the former suffers from the inherent domain shift between kinematic generation and physics-based tracking, while the latter struggles with the substantial modality gap between textual commands and low-level actions, limiting effective semantic alignment. Notably, humanoid states encode rich motion dynamics that are more semantically aligned with textual descriptions than low-level actions, making them a natural basis for deriving behavioral intent. Building upon this insight, we propose MIND, a novel end-to-end diffusion framework for text-driven physics-based humanoid control that leverages behavioral intent as a semantic bridge between textual commands and low-level actions. At its core, MIND introduces a multi-scale intent diffusion mechanism, where a holistic intent predictor captures global behavioral dynamics to guide overall behavior synthesis, while an immediate intent predictor provides step-wise, fine-grained signals for local behavior refinement at each diffusion step. This hierarchical intent formulation imposes a structured inductive bias for humanoid control, improving semantic alignment and behavioral naturalness. Furthermore, MIND encodes humanoid states into a latent space to enable more effective semantic intent modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MIND outperforms existing methods and synthesizes coherent, physically plausible, and semantically aligned humanoid behaviors from text commands. Our code will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [65] [PEDESTRIANQA: A Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Pedestrian Intention and Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24562)
*Naman Mishra,Shankar Gangisetty,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PedestrianQA数据集，将行人意图和轨迹预测转化为带有结构化推理解释的问答任务，使视觉大语言模型能够统一地进行预测并生成可解释性理由，实验表明微调后的VLM在多个基准上显著提升了预测准确性和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人意图和轨迹预测是自动驾驶安全部署的关键，现有方法通常需要为不同任务设计专门架构，且缺乏可解释性。因此，作者希望利用大视觉语言模型（VLM）的强大视觉理解和自然语言推理能力，构建一个统一且可解释的行人行为预测框架。

Method: 构建大规模视频数据集PedestrianQA，将行人意图和轨迹预测形式化为问答任务，并附带结构化推理解释（rationales）；对多种最先进的VLM进行微调，使其同时学习视觉动态、上下文线索和交通智能体交互，生成预测结果及其自然语言解释。

Result: 在PIE、JAAD、TITAN和IDD-PeD四个基准数据集上，微调后的VLM在意图分类、轨迹预测准确性以及推理解释质量方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: VLM作为统一且可解释的框架在安全关键的行人行为建模中具有强大潜力，能够在不依赖专门架构的前提下同时提升预测性能和解释能力。

Abstract: Pedestrian intention and trajectory prediction are critical for the safe deployment of autonomous driving systems, directly influencing navigation decisions in complex traffic environments. Recent advances in large vision-language models offer a powerful new paradigm for these tasks by combining high-capacity visual understanding with flexible natural language reasoning. In this work, we introduce PedestrianQA, a large-scale video-based dataset that formulates pedestrian intention and trajectory prediction as question-answering tasks augmented with structured rationales. PedestrianQA expresses richly annotated pedestrian sequences, in natural language, enabling VLMs to learn from visual dynamics, contextual cues, and interactions among traffic agents while generating concise explanations of their predictions without needing specialized architectures tailored for each task. Empirical evaluations across PIE, JAAD, TITAN, and IDD-PeD show that finetuning state-of-the-art VLMs on PedestrianQA significantly improves intention classification, trajectory forecasting accuracy, and the quality of explanatory rationales, demonstrating the strong potential of VLMs as a unified and explainable framework for safety-critical pedestrian behavior modeling.

</details>


### [66] [SAM3-Assisted Training of Lightweight YOLO Models for Precision Pig Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25860)
*Marcos Vinicius Mendes Faria,Thiago Borges Pereira,Isabella C. F. S. Condotta,Thiago Meireles Paixão,Francisco de Assis Boldt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全自动知识蒸馏流水线，利用SAM 3生成零样本伪标签来训练高效的YOLOv8检测器，在PigLife数据集上无人工标注即达到79.4% mAP，推理速度相比教师模型提升约200倍，证明了基础模型可作为零标注成本的监督器用于智慧农业的边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能基础模型（如SAM 3）计算量过大无法边缘部署，而轻量模型（如YOLO）需要大量人工标注成本，两者之间存在关键瓶颈，亟需一种无需人工标注的高效检测方案。

Method: 提出全自动知识蒸馏流水线：将SAM 3作为离线自动标注器生成零样本伪标签，用于训练YOLOv8检测器，无需任何人工标注干预。

Result: SAM 3训练的YOLOv8m达到79.4% mAP（无人工标注），推理延迟相比教师模型降低约200倍；低遮挡场景下AP₅₀超过99%，与人工标注基准相当。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以作为有效的零标注成本监督器，为智慧农业中的可扩展边缘计算解决方案提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detection has revolutionized Precision Livestock Farming (PLF), yet a critical barrier remains: high-performance Foundation Models (such as SAM 3) are too computationally intensive for edge deployment, while lightweight models (like YOLO) require prohibitive manual annotation efforts. This work proposes a fully automated knowledge distillation pipeline that leverages the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) to generate zero-shot pseudo-labels for training efficient YOLOv8 detectors. By treating SAM 3 as an offline auto-annotator, we eliminate the manual labeling bottleneck, producing models capable of real-time inference on resource-constrained hardware. We systematically evaluate this approach on the PigLife dataset, comparing SAM 3-supervised models against human-annotated baselines. Results demonstrate that a SAM 3-trained YOLOv8m achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP) of 79.4% without human intervention, while reducing inference latency by approximately 200$\times$ compared to the teacher model. Furthermore, stratified analysis reveals that in low-occlusion scenarios, the automated pipeline achieves detection rates comparable to human benchmarks ($AP_{50} > 99\%$). These findings indicate that foundation models can serve as effective, zero-annotation-cost supervisors, enabling scalable edge computing solutions for smart agriculture.

</details>


### [67] [SRUG: Shadow-Guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24700)
*Yonghao Zhao,Zexin Yin,Jian Yang,Beibei Wang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRUG是一个利用阴影引导3D补全模型恢复不可见区域几何结构，并结合迭代材质分解与物理光照模型，实现城市场景可重照明的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市场景通常无界且超出可见区域，不可见区域仍会向可见区域投射阴影，而稀疏输入视图和复杂光照条件导致材质分解存在严重歧义，现有方法难以合理建模不可见区域的阴影及实现可靠重照明。

Method: 1) 利用阴影引导3D补全模型恢复不可见区域几何；2) 采用迭代材质分解方案，借助大规模材质模型(LMM)提供材质监督并逐步分解场景材质属性；3) 引入基于物理的光照模型以捕捉复杂光照并支持可靠重照明。

Result: 大量定量评估和视觉对比表明，SRUG在新视角合成和重照明任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SRUG通过阴影引导几何补全和迭代材质分解有效解决了城市场景中不可见区域阴影建模和材质分解歧义问题，实现了物理合理的重照明效果。

Abstract: Creating relightable urban scenes from images or videos is widely useful but highly ill-posed. Urban environments are typically unbounded and extend beyond the visible regions. As a result, many portions of the scene remain unobserved, yet these invisible regions can cast shadows onto visible areas. Reasonably modeling shadows cast by such invisible regions is challenging and poses a significant obstacle to creating relightable urban scenes. At the same time, sparse input views and complex illumination conditions further complicate relighting, as they introduce severe ambiguities in material decomposition. In this paper, we propose Shadow-guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation model (SRUG), a novel framework designed to address relighting challenges in urban scenes. SRUG leverages shadows to guide a 3D completion model for recovering the geometry of invisible regions, promoting the synthesis of physically reasonable shadows. In addition, SRUG employs an iterative material decomposition scheme that applies the large material model (LMM) to provide material supervision and iteratively decompose the scene's material properties, enabling robust material decomposition. Building upon these components, we introduce a physically-based lighting model that captures the complex illumination of urban scenes and supports reliable relighting. Extensive quantitative evaluations and visual comparisons demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both novel view synthesis and relighting tasks.

</details>


### [68] [DRFusion: Drift-Resilient Temporally Consistent Infrared-Visible Video Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25775)
*Xingyuan Li,Haoyuan Xu,Shulin Li,Xiang Chen,Zhiying Jiang,Jinyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种抗漂移的红外与可见光视频融合方法，通过将任务重构为历史条件运动生成，引入稳定历史引导和软时间锚定作为光谱滤波，以及解耦结构-运动适应策略，实现了在融合质量和时间稳定性上的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在红外与可见光视频融合中面临时间一致性难题：光流法存在几何刚性和伪影问题，而基于扩散的逐帧融合在自回归扩展时缺乏时间约束，导致误差累积和漂移。

Method: 1) 将视频融合重构为历史条件运动生成；2) 提出稳定历史引导(Stabilized History Guidance)和软时间锚定(Soft Temporal Anchoring)，将时间一致性转化为光谱滤波，隐式聚合运动动态；3) 提出解耦结构-运动适应(Decoupled Structure-Motion Adaptation)策略，通过两阶段训练和潜在空间精炼桥接预训练先验与结构约束。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在融合质量和时间稳定性上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过隐式运动建模和去耦训练策略，有效解决了视频融合中的误差累积和时间漂移问题，无需刚性对齐即可实现稳定的时间一致性。

Abstract: Infrared and visible video fusion is essential for achieving comprehensive perception in dynamic scenes. However, maintaining temporal consistency remains a formidable challenge. Conventional methods relying on optical flow often suffer from geometric rigidity and ghosting artifacts. Moreover, standard diffusion-based fusion models typically operate in a frame-by-frame manner; when extended to autoregressive settings, they lack intrinsic temporal constraints and are prone to severe error accumulation and drifting, where minor artifacts amplify over time. To address these limitations, we propose a drift-resilient video fusion method that reformulates the task as history-conditioned motion generation. We introduce Stabilized History Guidance and Soft Temporal Anchoring to reframe temporal consistency as spectral filtering, implicitly aggregating motion dynamics without rigid alignment. Furthermore, our Decoupled Structure-Motion Adaptation strategy bridges pre-trained priors and structural constraints via two-stage training and latent refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both fusion quality and temporal stability.

</details>


### [69] [FDDet: Achieving Data-Efficient Food Defect Detection Under Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24508)
*Ruihao Xu,Yong Liu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-48数据集（涵盖13种食品、48个缺陷类别的细粒度标注）和FDDet半监督检测框架（含BBoxMixUp数据增强和CGPC伪标签校准），在数据稀缺场景下显著优于主流检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品缺陷检测研究缺乏统一基准且面临数据稀缺问题，亟需一个标准化的数据集和高效的低样本学习方法。

Method: 构建FDD-48数据集；提出FDDet半监督框架，包含：(1) BBoxMixUp——同类别缺陷区域混合增强以减少伪特征关联，(2) CGPC——基于样本内一致性的伪标签过滤校准机制。

Result: FDDet在FDD-48数据集上显著优于主流检测器，验证了其在数据有限场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 标准化基准FDD-48与半监督方法FDDet为解决食品缺陷检测中的数据稀缺问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Food defect detection is critical for automated quality control, yet existing studies lack unified benchmarks and suffer from data scarcity. We introduce FDD-48, a comprehensive dataset with fine-grained annotations across 13 food types and 48 defect categories under diverse real-world conditions. To improve detection with limited labeled data, we propose FDDet, a semi-supervised framework featuring two key components: (1) BBoxMixUp, a data augmentation technique that mixes same-category defect regions to reduce spurious feature associations, and (2) CGPC (Consistency-Guided Pseudo-Label Calibration), which filters pseudo-labels based on intra-sample consistency. Experiments show FDDet significantly outperforms mainstream detectors on FDD-48, demonstrating its effectiveness for food defect detection under data-limited scenarios.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Anatomically Plausible Human Image Generation via Synthetic Localized Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25759)
*Bao Li,Yuliang Xiu,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASAP框架，通过局部退化机制构建受控人体偏好对，结合局部化DPO变体，有效减少文本到图像模型生成人体的解剖结构错误，并创建了HAP数据集和HAF-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像基础模型在生成人体图像时存在解剖结构错误。监督微调依赖有限的高质量人像数据且受光照、姿态等混杂因素干扰；标准DPO无法利用解剖伪影的局部性特征。

Method: 提出ASAP框架：(1) 局部退化机制——在高质量人体图像的目标区域引入显式解剖错误，构建受控偏好对；(2) 创建HAP数据集（10K+偏好对）；(3) 提出局部化带边界的DPO变体，优先优化目标解剖区域并设置有限偏好间隔防止过度优化；(4) 构建HAF-Bench系统评估基准。

Result: 在多个基础模型上，ASAP持续减少人体解剖结构错误，同时保持整体图像质量。

Conclusion: 通过局部受控偏好对齐，能有效解决人体生成中的解剖正确性问题，优于全局DPO方法。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image foundation models have achieved remarkable visual realism, yet generating human images with correct anatomical structures remains challenging. Existing approaches enforce anatomical constraints through part-specific modules or localized loss weighting during supervised fine-tuning on high-quality human photos, but such datasets are limited and often provide ambiguous optimization signals due to confounding factors such as lighting, pose, and background. Preference-based alignment offers an alternative, but standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) treats all pixels equally and therefore fails to exploit the localized nature of anatomical artifacts. To address this, we propose the framework of Alignment via Synthetic Anatomical Preference (ASAP), which constructs controlled preference pairs through a localized degradation mechanism applied to high-fidelity human images. This mechanism performs a controlled experiment on images by introducing explicit anatomical errors in targeted regions while preserving the remaining content. With this mechanism, we create the Human Anatomical Preference (HAP) dataset with over 10K curated pairs for effective anatomical alignment of text-to-image human image generative models. To better leverage the locality of these controlled preference pairs, we introduce a localized and margin-bounded variant of DPO that prioritizes optimization in targeted anatomical regions while enforcing a finite preference margin to prevent over-optimization and preserve global semantics. We further introduce HAF-Bench, a benchmark for systematic evaluation of anatomical fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ASAP consistently reduces anatomical errors across multiple foundation models while maintaining overall image quality.

</details>


### [71] [Trust-Aware Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy for Robust Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25119)
*Xi Ding,Lei Wang,Syuan-Hao Li,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信任感知的域自适应框架，通过联合特征-预测差异（JFPD）度量，结合不确定性感知信任和语义对齐信任，在域自适应中实现了对特征和预测信号可靠性的建模，显著提升了迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域自适应方法仅在特征空间或预测空间单一视角下估计域差异，忽略了域偏移下特征和预测信号可能同时变得不可靠的问题，对所有目标样本等同对待会导致误导性对齐和次优迁移。

Method: 提出了联合特征-预测差异（JFPD）度量，统一表征表征差异和预测差异，并通过两种互补的信任机制加权：基于预测熵的不确定性感知信任（抑制不可靠预测）和基于特征空间中原型相似性的语义对齐信任（强调良好对齐的表征）。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架持续取得更优的域自适应性能，且所提出的差异估计与目标域误差具有良好的相关性。

Conclusion: 本文首次在域自适应中建模特征与预测之间交互中的信任重要性，通过可靠性感知的域差异估计引导自适应过程聚焦于目标域的可信区域。

Abstract: Domain adaptation aims to mitigate performance degradation caused by distribution shifts between a labeled source domain and an unlabeled or sparsely labeled target domain. Most existing approaches estimate domain discrepancy either in feature space or in prediction space. However, these single-perspective strategies overlook a critical problem under domain shift: the reliability of the signals used for alignment. In practice, both learned representations and semantic predictions may become unreliable, and treating all target samples equally can lead to misleading alignment and suboptimal transfer. We introduce trust-aware domain adaptation, a principled framework that models domain discrepancy through the reliability of feature and prediction signals. Central to our approach is the Joint Feature-Prediction Discrepancy (JFPD), a unified formulation that jointly captures representation divergence and prediction divergence while weighting their contributions by sample-specific trust. Trust is quantified via two complementary mechanisms: uncertainty-aware trust, derived from prediction entropy to suppress unreliable predictions, and semantic-alignment trust, computed from prototype similarity in feature space to emphasize well-aligned representations. By prioritizing confident and semantically consistent samples while down-weighting noisy or ambiguous ones, JFPD provides a reliability-aware estimate of domain discrepancy. We further integrate JFPD into a training objective that guides adaptation toward trustworthy regions of the target domain. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves superior adaptation performance and yields discrepancy estimates that correlate with target-domain error. This work addresses, for the first time, the importance of modeling trust in the interaction between features and predictions for domain adaptation.

</details>


### [72] [Resolving Ambiguity in Composed Image Retrieval via Calibrated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24634)
*Amsisan Tran,Baogh Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了组合图像检索（CIR）任务，指出现有方法忽略了查询的固有模糊性（如“make it more formal”对应的是图像区域而非单一图像），并提出了基于共形预测的校准意图解析框架，在保证覆盖率的条件下返回候选集，并通过信息增益策略主动询问最有效的澄清问题。作者构建了AmbiCIR基准和用户模拟器，实验表明该方法在单轮检索中达到SOTA，同时在少量交互下高效定位目标，首次实现了该任务的有效覆盖率和校准度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR方法普遍假设一个查询对应单一目标图像，并用Recall@K评估，但这忽略了查询的固有模糊性（如一描述对应多个可能图像），导致著名的假阴性问题，且模型无法区分精确查询与模糊查询。

Method: 将CIR重新定义为不确定性下的校准意图解析：在检索器外包装共形预测层，输出具有覆盖率保证的候选集，候选集大小作为模糊性的量化指标；当集合较大时，基于期望信息增益策略，从可解释的模糊性轴中选择最有效的澄清问题，使候选集收缩。

Result: 在开放域和时尚基准上，单轮检索达到SOTA；在交互式场景中，以远少于朴素对话基线的交互次数定位到目标图像；首次报告了该任务的有效覆盖率和校准指标。

Conclusion: 校准解析在精确查询上无性能损失，而对模糊查询能高效通过少量澄清交互达成目标，验证了不确定性感知建模对CIR的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) searches a corpus with a reference image and a text describing how to modify it. Despite rapid progress from triplet-trained compositors to zero-shot and generative methods, essentially all systems share one assumption: that a query maps to a single target, scored by Recall@K against one annotation. We argue this is fundamentally at odds with the task. A query such as make it more formal does not name an image but a region of the corpus, and which member the user intends is genuinely underdetermined. This underspecification is the root of the well-known false-negative problem and leaves current models unable to tell a precise query from an ambiguous one. We reframe CIR as calibrated intent resolution under uncertainty: a retriever is wrapped in a conformal prediction layer that returns a candidate set with a coverage guarantee and whose size is a principled measure of ambiguity; when the set is large, an expected-information-gain policy asks the single most useful clarifying question, drawn from interpretable ambiguity axes, and the set contracts. We introduce AmbiCIR, a benchmark and human-validated user simulator that revive the dormant auxiliary and dialogue annotations of CIRR and extend the multiple-positive setting of CIRCO. Across open-domain and fashion benchmarks our method matches single-turn state of the art, confirming calibrated resolution is cost-free on precise queries, while reaching the intended target in a fraction of the interaction budget required by naive conversational baselines, and it is the first to report valid coverage and calibration for the task.

</details>


### [73] [AI-T2I: Aggregating-and-Isolating Cross-Attention to Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25763)
*Shipeng Cao,Biao Qian,Haipeng Liu,Yang Wang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-T2I方法，通过聚合-隔离交叉注意力机制解决扩散模型在文本到图像生成中存在的子令牌内激活分散和子令牌间激活重叠问题，实现精确的文本-图像对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在文本到图像合成中存在交叉注意力图对齐不精确的问题。已有工作主要关注不同主体令牌之间的激活重叠，而忽略了同一主体令牌内部的激活分散问题。

Method: 提出聚合-隔离交叉注意力方法(AI-T2I)：1) 聚合损失：识别并整合分散的子令牌内激活，同时隐式缓解潜在的重叠问题；2) 隔离损失：将不同子令牌之间的激活推离，实现精确的文本-图像对齐。

Result: 在多个基准测试上超越现有最先进方法，并在可控布局生成和个性化生成等下游任务上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AI-T2I通过同时解决令牌内激活分散和令牌间激活重叠问题，有效提升了扩散模型在文本到图像合成中的对齐精度，且具有良好的跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Text-to-image synthesis has made significant progress, benefiting from the strong generative capabilities of diffusion models.
